                                <script>const apiUrl = '/apis/v4-raw/crossroad/api/v0/crossroad';
    const CARD_PAGE_SIZE = 15;
    const TABLE_PAGE_SIZE = 25;
    const LEGACY_PROJECTS_VIEW_KEY = '@projects-list-view';
    const FOLDERS_VIEW_MODE_KEY = 'xr:foldersViewMode';

    if (localStorage.getItem(FOLDERS_VIEW_MODE_KEY) === null) {
        try {
            const legacy = localStorage.getItem(LEGACY_PROJECTS_VIEW_KEY);
            if (legacy !== null) {
                const mode = legacy === 'table' ? 'table' : 'card';
                localStorage.setItem(FOLDERS_VIEW_MODE_KEY, JSON.stringify(mode));
                localStorage.removeItem(LEGACY_PROJECTS_VIEW_KEY);
            }
        } catch { }
    }
    const isCardView = localStorage.getItem(FOLDERS_VIEW_MODE_KEY) !== '"table"'
    const limit = isCardView ? CARD_PAGE_SIZE : TABLE_PAGE_SIZE;

    const handleResponse = (response) => {
        if (response.ok) {
            return response.json();
        }

        throw new Error(response.statusText);
    }
    const headers = {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    }

    const foldersMergeStatsPromise = fetch(`${apiUrl}/merge_stats`, {
        headers,
    }).then(handleResponse);

    const foldersPromise = fetch(`${apiUrl}/list`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers,
        body: JSON.stringify({
            filter: isCardView ? {} : { domain: { mode: 2 } },
            range: {
                limit,
                offset: 0
            }
        })
    }).then(handleResponse);

    const toolkitListPromise = foldersPromise.then(({ folders = [] }) => {
        if (folders.length === 0) {
            return { folders, toolkits: [], downgradeAt: null };
        }

        return fetch(`${apiUrl}/toolkits`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers,
            body: JSON.stringify({
                folders: folders.map(folder => folder.id),
                withPromo: isCardView
            })
        }).then(handleResponse)
    });
